## 描繪構建區 (制圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**english**　Delineate Built-Up Areas

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html

**id**　b686dd73cbb5a92d62-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Delineate Built-Up Areas，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入建筑物圖層 [in_buildings]、標識符欄位 （可選） [identifier_field]、邊圖徵 （可選） [edge_features]、分組距離 [grouping_distance]、最小細節層次大小 [minimum_detail_size]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、最小建筑物計數 （可選） [minimum_building_count]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html#parameters

## 描繪構建區 (制圖工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Delineate Built-Up Areas

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 概化工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html

**id**　b686dd73cbb5a92d62-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/cartography/delineate-built-up-areas.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_buildings", "label_zh": "輸入建筑物圖層", "label_en": "Input Building Layers", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The layers containing buildings with density and arrangement that are used to define appropriate output built-up polygons. Multiple building layers can be assessed simultaneously. Building features can be points or polygons."}, {"parameter": "identifier_field", "label_zh": "標識符欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Identifier Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "A field in the input feature classes that will hold a status code indicating whether the input feature is part of the resulting built-up area . This field must be either short or long integer type and common to all input layers if multiple input layers are used. 0—The building is not represented by an output built-up area polygon. 1—The building is represented by an output built-up area polygon and is within the resulting polygon. 2—The building is represented by an output built-up area polygon and is outside the resulting polygon."}, {"parameter": "edge_features", "label_zh": "邊圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Edge Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The layers that will be used to define the edges of the built-up area polygons. Typically, these are roads, but other common examples are rivers, coastlines, and administrative areas. Built-up area polygons snap to an edge feature if one is generally aligned with the trend of the polygon edge and within the grouping distance away. Edge features can be lines or polygons."}, {"parameter": "grouping_distance", "label_zh": "分組距離", "label_en": "Grouping Distance", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "Buildings closer together than the grouping distance are considered collectively as candidates for representation by an output built-up area polygon. This distance is measured from the edges of polygon buildings and the centers of point buildings."}, {"parameter": "minimum_detail_size", "label_zh": "最小細節層次大小", "label_en": "Minimum Detail Size", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The relative degree of detail in the output built-up area polygons. This is approximately to the minimum allowable diameter of a hole or cavity in the built-up area polygon. The actual size and shape of holes and cavities within the polygon is determined also by the arrangement of the input buildings, the grouping distance, and the presence of edge features if they are used."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class containing built-up area polygons representing clustered arrangements of input buildings."}, {"parameter": "minimum_building_count", "label_zh": "最小建筑物計數 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Building Count (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The minimum number of buildings that must be collectively considered for representation by an output built-up area polygon. The default value is 4. The minimum building count must be greater than or equal to 0."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.cartography.DelineateBuiltUpAreas(in_buildings, {identifier_field}, {edge_features}, grouping_distance, minimum_detail_size, out_feature_class, {minimum_building_count})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_buildings、identifier_field、edge_features、grouping_distance、minimum_detail_size、out_feature_class、minimum_building_count。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 001988: 子類型使用域，無法刪除。 · 描述

**id**　b69d3322ba8599a091-1

**english**　001988: The domain is used by a subtype and cannot be deleted.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1901-2000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001988.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001988.html

**text**　您嘗試刪除的域正被子類型使用。 如果某個域正在使用中，則無法刪除該域。
如果要刪除域，則不得將該域分配到表（無論是否具有子類型）中的任何欄位，且該域不可以為公共設施網絡中的必需域。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001988.html#description

**content_version**　3.7

## 視頻元資料至圖徵類 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　b6a8f76dad51f544fb-2

**english**　Video Metadata To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 動態視頻影像

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html

**text**　此工具可將視頻元資料導出為圖徵類，其中所有的元資料都將作為屬性表中的欄位導出。 這些圖徵可用于平臺位置、視頻幀輪廓和視頻幀中心，以驗證正確的資料覆蓋范圍、執行空間查詢和執行屬性查詢，以快速查找感興趣的視頻幀。
此工具僅適用于存檔視頻文件。 不支持實時流視頻。
必須將兼容元資料的視頻文件用作輸入。 如果視頻不包含視頻流中的元資料，則該工具將失敗。
該工具支持五種類型的輸出，您必須至少指定一種輸出。 如果全部五種輸出均留空，則該工具將運行失敗。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html#usage

min_time_between = "20 Seconds"

    in_video, out_metadata, flight_path, flight_path_type, image_path,
    image_path_type, image_footprint, start_time, stop_time, distance_between,
    min_time_between)

## 視頻元資料至圖徵類 (影像分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 動態視頻影像

**english**　Video Metadata To Feature Class

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html

**id**　b6a8f76dad51f544fb-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Video Metadata To Feature Class，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入視頻 [in_video]、輸出元資料文件 （可選） [csv_file]、輸出飛行路徑圖徵類 （可選） [flightpath]、飛行路徑圖徵類類型 （可選） [flightpath_type]、輸出圖像路徑圖徵類 （可選） [imagepath]、圖像路徑圖徵類類型 （可選） [imagepath_type]、輸出圖像覆蓋區圖徵類 （可選） [footprint]、元資料捕獲開始時間 （可選） [start_time]、元資料捕獲停止時間 （可選） [stop_time]、圖徵之間的最小距離 （可選） [min_distance]、圖徵之間的最短時間 （可選） [min_time]、輸出 VMTI 圖徵資料集 （可選） [vmti]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/video-metadata-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_video", "label_zh": "輸入視頻", "label_en": "Input Video", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The metadata compliant input video file containing essential metadata for each frame of the video data. The supported video file types are .av1, .m2ts, .mkv, .mov, .mp2, .mp4, .mpeg, .mpeg2, .mpeg4, .mpg, .mpg2, .mpg4, .ps, .ts, .vob, and .wmv."}, {"parameter": "csv_file", "label_zh": "輸出元資料文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Metadata File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "An output .csv or .json file containing metadata about the video frames for specific times. The metadata file is in the same format used by the Video Multiplexer tool."}, {"parameter": "flightpath", "label_zh": "輸出飛行路徑圖徵類 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Flight Path Feature Class (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The feature class containing the platform's flight path information."}, {"parameter": "flightpath_type", "label_zh": "飛行路徑圖徵類類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Flight Path Feature Class Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the feature class type that will be used for the flight path. Point—A point feature class will be used for the flight path. Polyline—A polyline feature class will be used for the flight path. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "imagepath", "label_zh": "輸出圖像路徑圖徵類 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Image Path Feature Class (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class containing the image path information."}, {"parameter": "imagepath_type", "label_zh": "圖像路徑圖徵類類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Path Feature Class Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the feature class type that will be used for the image path. If you're using a point output, the center of each video frame image will appear on the map. Point—A point feature class will be used for the image path. Polyline—A polyline feature class will be used for the image path. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "footprint", "label_zh": "輸出圖像覆蓋區圖徵類 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Image Footprint Feature Class (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class containing the video image footprint information."}, {"parameter": "start_time", "label_zh": "元資料捕獲開始時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Metadata Capture Start Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Time Unit; Date", "description_en": "The metadata recording start time from the beginning of the video. The input format is d.hh:mm:ss, and the default start time is 0.00:00:00. Metadata time stamps are not used in this field; the time of the video file is used."}, {"parameter": "stop_time", "label_zh": "元資料捕獲停止時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Metadata Capture Stop Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Time Unit; Date", "description_en": "The metadata recording end time. The input format is d.hh:mm:ss. If no value is provided, the value will default to the end of the video. Metadata time stamps are not used in this field."}, {"parameter": "min_distance", "label_zh": "圖徵之間的最小距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Distance Between Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance between the features in sequential video frames. If no value is provided, every metadata feature will be extracted and added to the feature class."}, {"parameter": "min_time", "label_zh": "圖徵之間的最短時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Time Between Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Time Unit", "description_en": "The time interval between the features in sequential video frames. If no value is provided, every metadata feature will be extracted and added to the feature class."}, {"parameter": "vmti", "label_zh": "輸出 VMTI 圖徵資料集 （可選）", "label_en": "Output VMTI Feature Dataset (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Dataset", "description_en": "The output feature dataset containing the video VMTI information."}]

**signatures**　
- VideoMetadataToFeatureClass(in_video, {csv_file}, {flightpath}, {flightpath_type}, {imagepath}, {imagepath_type}, {footprint}, {start_time}, {stop_time}, {min_distance}, {min_time}, {vmti})

## Generate Threshold Drive Times (Business Analyst Tools) · 用法

**id**　b6adcbfcc2985ae89e-2

**english**　Generate Threshold Drive Times

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/generate-threshold-drive-times.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/generate-threshold-drive-times.html

**text**　The size of output polygons is determined by the Threshold Values parameter value provided for a selected Threshold Variable parameter value from the Business Analyst dataset.
The Threshold Variable parameter accepts only variables of Count type. Median, Average, Percent, or Index cannot be used.
Multiple threshold drive time polygons can be created around each input by typing individual values into separate threshold value text boxes.
The ID Field parameter uniquely identifies the point location around which threshold drive time polygons will be created.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/generate-threshold-drive-times.html#usage
